# Longpath test suite. Opt-in via -DLONGPATH_BUILD_TESTS=ON.
#
# Test targets compile against LongpathObjs (the OBJECT library that backs
# the main executable) so behavior under test is byte-identical to shipped
# code. Each test gets its own thin executable + add_test() registration.
#
# CI parallel sharding (added 2026-05-13, Linux speed-alignment epic):
# When -DLONGPATH_TEST_SHARDS=N -DLONGPATH_TEST_SHARD=I is passed (0 ≤ I < N)
# the function below registers only the slice of tests whose name-MD5
# falls in bucket I.  .github/workflows/ci.yml uses this to fan the
# 451-test build across 4 parallel runners; each shard builds the main
# app + ~113 tests + runs them in roughly the same wall-clock as the
# macOS / Windows app-only builds.  Distribution is content-hashed so
# adding/removing a single test reshuffles at most a handful between
# buckets — predictable churn, no manual bucket maintenance.
#
# Default (LONGPATH_TEST_SHARDS unset or 0): sharding disabled, every
# longpath_add_test() registers normally.  Local developer builds keep
# the full suite.

find_package(Qt6 REQUIRED COMPONENTS Test)

# Sharding inputs (CI-only; default to "off" for local dev builds).
# Validated once here so a typo in the matrix YAML fails configure
# loudly instead of silently running every shard's full suite.
set(LONGPATH_TEST_SHARDS "0" CACHE STRING "Total test shard count (0 = no sharding)")
set(LONGPATH_TEST_SHARD  "0" CACHE STRING "This shard's index (0..LONGPATH_TEST_SHARDS-1)")
if(LONGPATH_TEST_SHARDS GREATER 0)
    if(LONGPATH_TEST_SHARD LESS 0 OR LONGPATH_TEST_SHARD GREATER_EQUAL LONGPATH_TEST_SHARDS)
        message(FATAL_ERROR
            "LONGPATH_TEST_SHARD=${LONGPATH_TEST_SHARD} out of range "
            "[0, ${LONGPATH_TEST_SHARDS}).")
    endif()
    message(STATUS
        "Test sharding enabled — building shard "
        "${LONGPATH_TEST_SHARD} of ${LONGPATH_TEST_SHARDS}")

    # One-line stub source shared by every sharded-out test target. The
    # stub OBJECT library never enters the build graph (EXCLUDE_FROM_ALL
    # + nothing depends on it), but it accepts the same target_*()
    # follow-up calls as a real test executable so the existing
    # per-test target_compile_definitions() sites in this file silently
    # no-op for skipped shards.
    set(LONGPATH_TEST_STUB_CPP "${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/_sharded_test_stub.cpp")
    file(WRITE "${LONGPATH_TEST_STUB_CPP}"
        "// Auto-generated by tests/CMakeLists.txt. Stub source for\n"
        "// sharded-out test targets. Never compiled, never linked.\n")
endif()

# ── Subsystem label derivation (2026-07-25) ────────────────────────────
# Scans a test's own #include lines and emits ctest LABELS for the
# subsystems it touches, so `ctest -L core` works without editing 513
# test files or hand-maintaining a mapping table.
#
# Matches both the common bare form (`#include "core/Foo.h"`, resolved
# via the include path) and the `../src/core/Foo.h` form used by the
# handful of tests that reach into src/ with an explicit relative path
# (e.g. tst_waterfall_scrollback.cpp, tst_parametric_eq_widget_*.cpp) —
# the optional `(src/)?` group was added empirically after an initial
# version without it mis-labelled all 6 of those tests "unclassified".
#
# Measured distribution across the suite: core ~83%, models ~38%,
# gui ~30% (tests commonly touch more than one, so these do not sum
# to 100).
function(_longpath_derive_test_labels out_var src_file)
    set(_labels "")
    if(NOT EXISTS "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/${src_file}")
        set(${out_var} "unclassified" PARENT_SCOPE)
        return()
    endif()
    file(STRINGS "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/${src_file}" _incs
         REGEX "^[ \t]*#include[ \t]+\"")
    foreach(_line IN LISTS _incs)
        if(_line MATCHES "\"(\\.\\./)*(src/)?core/")
            list(APPEND _labels "core")
        endif()
        if(_line MATCHES "\"(\\.\\./)*(src/)?models/")
            list(APPEND _labels "models")
        endif()
        if(_line MATCHES "\"(\\.\\./)*(src/)?gui/")
            list(APPEND _labels "gui")
        endif()
    endforeach()

    # ── Marke "appless": braucht dieser Pruefstand eine Oberflaeche? ──
    #
    # QTEST_APPLESS_MAIN heisst: kein QApplication, kein QWidget, kein
    # Schirm -- reine Kernlogik. QTEST_MAIN heisst das Gegenteil.
    # Dieselbe Herleitung wie oben, aus der Datei selbst gelesen statt
    # aus einer Tabelle, die jemand pflegen muss.
    #
    # Wozu (2026-10-22): die Windows-CI faehrt genau diese Teilmenge.
    # Dass ein Pruefstand mit Oberflaeche dort traegt, ist eine eigene
    # Frage -- auf Linux braucht es dafuer xvfb, auf macOS das
    # offscreen-Plugin, und auf Windows ist beides nicht geprueft. Eine
    # BENANNTE Teilmenge, die laeuft, ist mehr als eine ganze Reihe,
    # die es nicht tut.
    #
    # `ctest -L appless` faehrt sie, `tests_appless` baut sie -- das
    # Ziel entsteht wie jedes andere Markenziel weiter unten.
    file(STRINGS "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/${src_file}" _appless
         REGEX "QTEST_APPLESS_MAIN")
    if(_appless)
        list(APPEND _labels "appless")
    endif()

    list(REMOVE_DUPLICATES _labels)
    if(NOT _labels)
        set(_labels "unclassified")
    endif()
    set(${out_var} "${_labels}" PARENT_SCOPE)
endfunction()

# ── Shared sandbox-init object library (2026-07-25) ────────────────────
# TestSandboxInit.cpp was previously listed as a source on all 513 test
# executables, so it was compiled 513 times (0.10s each, ~50 CPU-s of
# pure duplication).
#
# It MUST stay an OBJECT library, not a static library: its entire
# payload is a file-scope static constructor (g_nereusTestSandboxInit)
# with no referenced symbols, and a static-library link would drop it
# as unused. $<TARGET_OBJECTS:> forces the object in unconditionally,
# preserving the pre-main() QStandardPaths::setTestModeEnabled(true)
# guarantee that keeps ctest runs from overwriting the developer's real
# NereusSDR.settings (see the header comment in TestSandboxInit.cpp).
#
# Deliberately does NOT reuse the LongpathObjs PCH.  This is one 50-line
# TU whose only include is <QStandardPaths>, so the PCH buys nothing, and
# reusing it means compiling with a different -D set than the PCH was
# built with (this target links only Qt6::Core, not the HAVE_WDSP /
# HAVE_FFTW3 / LONGPATH_GPU_SPECTRUM PUBLIC defines the others inherit).
# AppleClang tolerates the mismatch; GCC silently drops the PCH and MSVC
# errors outright (C2855).
add_library(longpath_test_sandbox OBJECT EXCLUDE_FROM_ALL TestSandboxInit.cpp)
target_link_libraries(longpath_test_sandbox PRIVATE Qt6::Core)

# Schirm für die Prüfstände -- siehe die Begründung in
# longpath_add_test() weiter unten. Auf Windows leer, weil die dortige
# CI ohne offscreen grün ist.
if(WIN32)
    set(_longpath_default_test_platform "")
else()
    set(_longpath_default_test_platform "offscreen")
endif()
set(LONGPATH_TEST_PLATFORM "${_longpath_default_test_platform}" CACHE STRING
    "QT_QPA_PLATFORM für die Prüfstände; leer = Systemvorgabe")

# Zeitgrenze je Prüfstand, in Sekunden. Begründung bei
# set_tests_properties() in longpath_add_test() weiter unten.
# 120 bleibt die Vorgabe für Linux, macOS und jeden lokalen Bau.
set(LONGPATH_TEST_TIMEOUT "120" CACHE STRING
    "Zeitgrenze je Prüfstand in Sekunden (ctest TIMEOUT)")

function(longpath_add_test name)
    # Ein Test HINTER dem Sammelziel all_tests (unten) kommt nicht in
    # dessen Abhaengigkeiten: CI baut nur all_tests, ctest meldet ihn
    # dann als "Not Run" -- so am 2026-09-25 mit drei neuen Tests, die
    # ans Dateiende gehaengt waren. Laut abbrechen statt still fehlen.
    if(TARGET all_tests)
        message(FATAL_ERROR "longpath_add_test(${name}) steht hinter dem "
                "Sammelziel all_tests und wuerde im CI nie gebaut. Den "
                "Eintrag vor den Block 'Aggregate \"all_tests\" target' "
                "verschieben.")
    endif()
    # Sharding gate — when this test's hash bucket doesn't match the
    # active shard, register a stub OBJECT library (EXCLUDE_FROM_ALL,
    # backed by the shared stub source set up at the top of this file)
    # under the test name and bail.  The stub absorbs follow-up
    # target_compile_definitions(${name} ...) calls in this file
    # silently — OBJECT libs accept PRIVATE/PUBLIC/INTERFACE keywords,
    # so the existing call sites work unmodified — and EXCLUDE_FROM_ALL
    # plus zero dependents keep the stub out of the build graph.
    # Skipping add_test() means ctest never sees the name.  A stable
    # MD5 of the test name keeps bucket assignment deterministic
    # across runs and across PRs.
    if(LONGPATH_TEST_SHARDS GREATER 0)
        string(MD5 _hash "${name}")
        # Take the first 4 hex chars (16-bit) — plenty of entropy for
        # a handful of buckets, and well within math(EXPR)'s 64-bit
        # signed range.
        string(SUBSTRING "${_hash}" 0 4 _hash_prefix)
        math(EXPR _bucket "0x${_hash_prefix} % ${LONGPATH_TEST_SHARDS}")
        if(NOT _bucket EQUAL ${LONGPATH_TEST_SHARD})
            add_library(${name} OBJECT EXCLUDE_FROM_ALL "${LONGPATH_TEST_STUB_CPP}")
            return()
        endif()
    endif()

    # Additional source files may be passed as extra arguments (ARGN) —
    # needed by Phase 3I tests that link test-only fakes like P1FakeRadio.
    #
    # TestSandboxInit.cpp is always compiled in. Its global static
    # constructor calls QStandardPaths::setTestModeEnabled(true) before
    # main() runs, so AppSettings::instance() resolves to a sandbox
    # path instead of the real user config file. Without this, ctest
    # runs can (and have — see v0.1.1 alpha) silently wipe the
    # developer's NereusSDR.settings via P1FakeRadio-keyed test writes.
    # 2026-05-26 KG4VCF: EXCLUDE_FROM_ALL makes test executables NOT
    # part of the default `cmake --build build` target.  526 test
    # binaries × non-trivial link time = real dev-loop pain when a
    # single source change forces all of them to relink.  Devs who
    # want to run tests use:
    #
    #   cmake --build build --target tst_specific_name  # one test
    #   cmake --build build --target all_tests          # the lot
    #
    # CI uses `all_tests` followed by `ctest`.  Pre-commit hooks
    # currently don't invoke ctest (they're verification scripts,
    # not test runs), so nothing in the existing workflow breaks.
    #
    # The aggregate `all_tests` target is created at the bottom of
    # this file from a global property each longpath_add_test() call
    # appends to.
    add_executable(${name} EXCLUDE_FROM_ALL ${name}.cpp $<TARGET_OBJECTS:longpath_test_sandbox> ${ARGN})
    target_link_libraries(${name} PRIVATE LongpathObjs Qt6::Test)
    set_property(GLOBAL APPEND PROPERTY LONGPATH_ALL_TESTS ${name})

    # CMakeLists.txt's top-level `if(CMAKE_BUILD_TYPE STREQUAL "Debug")`
    # block already compiles LongpathObjs with -fsanitize=address (its
    # PUBLIC compile option, so every test executable linking against it
    # inherits the instrumentation) but only adds the matching LINK
    # option to the `Longpath` app target, not to test executables —
    # found 2026-08-30 while investigating a real, non-reproducing
    # SIGSEGV (docs/architecture/2026-08-29-sunsdr-tci-teardown-segfault-investigation.md):
    # a Debug-configured test build would have ASAN-instrumented object
    # code but no ASAN runtime linked in, which fails at link time
    # (undefined __asan_* symbols), not silently — but that means the
    # existing sanitizer support was never actually usable for tests at
    # all, only for the app binary. Mirrors the same Debug-only gate.
    if(NOT MSVC AND CMAKE_BUILD_TYPE STREQUAL "Debug")
        target_link_options(${name} PRIVATE -fsanitize=address)
    endif()

    # Reuse the LongpathObjs precompiled header (CMakeLists.txt §PCH).
    # 451 test executables × per-target PCH compile would burn the wins;
    # REUSE_FROM means the PCH is built once on LongpathObjs and shared
    # across every test target. Save 5 to 10 minutes of test compile
    # time on Linux CI. Gated on LONGPATH_USE_PCH so users who turn the
    # PCH off (e.g. for header-hygiene auditing) don't get a broken
    # link.
    if(LONGPATH_USE_PCH)
        target_precompile_headers(${name} REUSE_FROM LongpathObjs)
    endif()

    add_test(NAME ${name} COMMAND ${name})

    # Labels let `ctest -L core` request a subset; TIMEOUT converts a hung
    # test from an indefinite block into a failure. 120s is deliberately
    # generous: the slowest test in the suite as of 2026-07-25 is
    # tst_ps_feedback_channel at ~30s.
    #
    # ── Die Grenze ist jetzt einstellbar (2026-10-23) ────────────────
    #
    # Weil 120 s je Pruefstand auf einer Plattform, auf der VIELE
    # haengen, kein Zeitlimit mehr ist, sondern eine Multiplikation:
    # 189 Pruefstaende × 120 s sind 6,3 Stunden. Genau das ist auf
    # Windows passiert -- der ctest-Schritt des Laufs 39812571171 lief
    # ueber zweieinhalb Stunden und wurde abgebrochen, ohne ein
    # Ergebnis zu liefern. Ein Lauf, der in sein eigenes Zeitlimit
    # rennt, sagt NICHTS: er nennt nicht einmal die Namen der Haenger.
    #
    # `ctest --timeout` hilft dafuer nicht: diese Eigenschaft hier
    # schlaegt den Kommandozeilenwert. Die Grenze muss also beim
    # Konfigurieren fallen, und darum steht sie in einer
    # Cache-Variablen.
    _longpath_derive_test_labels(_test_labels "${name}.cpp")
    set_tests_properties(${name} PROPERTIES
        LABELS "${_test_labels}"
        TIMEOUT ${LONGPATH_TEST_TIMEOUT})

    # Per-label build aggregates, so `ctest -L core` has a matching
    # `tests_core` target that actually builds what it is about to run.
    #
    # Without these, the documented subset workflow is unsafe: test
    # executables are EXCLUDE_FROM_ALL, so a routine `cmake --build build`
    # does not rebuild them.  On a clean tree `ctest -L core` then reports
    # every selected test as Not Run, and -- worse -- on an already
    # populated build tree it silently runs STALE binaries and returns a
    # false green after a source change.  Codex review, PR #305.
    foreach(_label IN LISTS _test_labels)
        set_property(GLOBAL APPEND PROPERTY LONGPATH_TESTS_LABEL_${_label} ${name})
        set_property(GLOBAL APPEND PROPERTY LONGPATH_TEST_LABEL_NAMES ${_label})
    endforeach()
endfunction()

#     setPSAutomode, setPSTurnon, setPSControl, setPSLoopDelay, setPSMoxDelay,

#     Stream0/Stream1 RX/TX feedback wiring per cmaster.cs:533-534.
# Same does-not-crash strategy as B-1/B-2/B-3 PS smoke tests.  Wrappers
# null-guard on `txa[m_channelId].rsmpin.p == nullptr` so calls on a bare
# `TxChannel ch(1)` are safe in HAVE_WDSP-linked builds.

# ── Phase 3M-4 Task 4: PsFeedbackChannel wrapper (WDSP feedback RX channel) ──
# 4 tests covering channel-id uniqueness, sample-rate round-trip, activity
# counter, and distinctness from the existing TX channel id (1).  The

# ── P1 full-parity Task 2.1: P1RadioConnection::setLineInGain virtual ────────
# 12 tests pin the line_in_gain field (bank 11 C2 low 5 bits, mask 0x1F) in
# P1RadioConnection::setLineInGain():
#   1.  Default state: C2 low 5 bits = 0 (m_lineInGain default 0).
#   2.  setLineInGain(15) → C2 low 5 bits = 0x0F.
#   3.  setLineInGain(31) → C2 low 5 bits = 0x1F (max).
#   4.  setLineInGain(-5) → clamps to 0 (after first storing non-zero).
#   5.  setLineInGain(50) → clamps to 31.
#   6.  setLineInGain does NOT touch C2 bit 6 (puresignal_run cross-bit guard).
#   7.  Bank 11 C0 address remains 0x14.
#   8.  Round-trip: 0 → 23 → 0 produces correct bit values.
#   9.  setLineInGain sets m_forceBank11Next (Codex P2 flush pattern).
#  10.  Repeated same-value call still sets the flush flag.
#  11.  Idempotency: setLineInGain(15) twice doesn't crash.
#  12.  Codec path (HL2): C2 emits 0 unconditionally — documents intentional
#       codec divergence (HL2 has no mic jack); flush flag still fires.
# Source: Thetis ChannelMaster/networkproto1.c:600 [v2.10.3.13].
longpath_add_test(tst_p1_line_in_gain_setter)
target_compile_definitions(tst_p1_line_in_gain_setter PRIVATE LONGPATH_BUILD_TESTS)

# ── P1 full-parity Task 2.2: P1RadioConnection::setUserDigOut virtual ────────
# 13 tests pin the user_dig_out field (bank 11 C3 low 4 bits, mask 0x0F) in
# P1RadioConnection::setUserDigOut():
#   1.  Default state: C3 low 4 bits = 0 (m_userDigOut default 0).
#   2.  setUserDigOut(0) → C3 low 4 bits = 0 (explicit zero).
#   3.  setUserDigOut(0x0A) → C3 low 4 bits = 0x0A (alternating bits).
#   4.  setUserDigOut(0x0F) → C3 low 4 bits = 0x0F (max).
#   5.  setUserDigOut(0xFF) → C3 = 0x0F (high-nibble masked off).
#   6.  Cross-bit guard: setUserDigOut leaves C1 (mic bits) unchanged.
#   7.  Cross-bit guard: setUserDigOut leaves C2 (line_in/puresignal) unchanged.
#   8.  Bank 11 C0 address remains 0x14.
#   9.  Round-trip: 0 → 0x07 → 0 produces correct bit values.
#  10.  setUserDigOut sets m_forceBank11Next (Codex P2 flush pattern).
#  11.  Repeated same-value call still sets the flush flag.
#  12.  Idempotency: setUserDigOut(0x0A) twice doesn't crash.
#  13.  Codec path (HL2): C3 emits 0 unconditionally — documents intentional
#       codec divergence (HL2 has no Penny header); flush flag still fires.
# Source: Thetis ChannelMaster/networkproto1.c:601 [v2.10.3.13].
longpath_add_test(tst_p1_user_dig_out_setter)
target_compile_definitions(tst_p1_user_dig_out_setter PRIVATE LONGPATH_BUILD_TESTS)

# ── P1 full-parity Task 2.3: P1RadioConnection::setPuresignalRun virtual ─────
# 12 tests pin the puresignal_run flag (bank 11 C2 bit 6, mask 0x40) in
# P1RadioConnection::setPuresignalRun():
#   1.  Default state: C2 bit 6 = 0 (m_puresignalRun default false).
#   2.  setPuresignalRun(false) → C2 bit 6 = 0 (explicit baseline).

#   6.  Cross-bit guard: leaves C3 (user_dig_out) unchanged.
#   7.  Bank 11 C0 address remains 0x14.

#  10.  Repeated same-value call still sets the flush flag.

#       codec divergence (HL2 has no on-radio PS path); flush flag still fires.

#   1.  No MOX: bank 2 emits RX freq even with PS armed.
#   2.  HermesII PS-MOX: bank 2 → TX freq override.
#   3.  HermesII PS-MOX: bank 3 → TX freq override.
#   4.  HermesII no-PS MOX: bank 3 emits RX2 freq (Hermes default path).

# ── PS pairing must not run during ordinary RX (2026-08-01) ──────────────
# PsDdcConfig defaulted psFbDdc/txMonDdc to 0 and 1, and only the PS-MOX
# branches assign them, so P1RadioConnection::parseEp6Frame's
# `m_psFbDdc >= 0 && m_psTxMonDdc >= 0` gate was open on every board from the
# first DDC config onward: two QVector copies plus one signal per EP6 frame,
# on the connection thread, all discarded by PsccPump's !m_active guard.
# These 4 tests pin the sentinel (-1 = no pair) in both directions: silent
# with PureSignal off or keyed without it, one emit on (DDC2, DDC3) under
# HL2 PS-MOX, and silent again after unkeying.

# ── P1 full-parity Task 2.5: TransmitModel::pureSig → setPuresignalRun wiring ─
# 8 tests pin the model-to-connection wiring installed in
# RadioModel::wireConnectionSignals + the initial-push pattern in
# RadioModel::connectToRadio.  Wire-byte side (bank 11 C2 bit 6) is
# already covered by tst_p1_puresignal_run_setter; these tests cover the
# higher-level model-layer wiring contract:
#   1.  Initial push: default model state → bank 11 C2 bit 6 clear.
#   2.  Initial push: persisted true → bank 11 C2 bit 6 = 0x40.
#   3.  Toggle on: model setter → wiring → connection setter → bit set.
#   4.  Toggle off: model false → wiring clears bit 6.
#   5.  Round-trip: false → true → false bit-tracks across the wiring.
#   6.  Idempotent setter: same value twice → wiring fires only on change.
#   7.  Cross-bit guard: pureSig wiring does not perturb C2 line_in_gain.
#   8.  loadFromSettings reads the pureSignal/enabled key.
#   8b. loadFromSettings defaults to false when key absent.
# Source: Thetis ChannelMaster/networkproto1.c:599-600 + PSForm.cs:240
#         [v2.10.3.13].
longpath_add_test(tst_radio_model_puresignal_run_wiring)
target_compile_definitions(tst_radio_model_puresignal_run_wiring PRIVATE LONGPATH_BUILD_TESTS)

# Tests the per-board PA calibration model: paCalBoardClassFor() mapping for
# every HPSDRModel value, defaults() factory tables for each board class, and
# interpolate() matching Thetis console.cs:6691-6768 CalibratedPAPower +
# PowerKernel [v2.10.3.13]:
#   - Board-class mapping: 17 cases covering every HPSDRModel + sentinels.
#   - defaults(): Anan10/Anan100/Anan8000/HermesLite cal tables match the
#     ud{10,100,200}PA{N}W spinbox label values + None returns all zeros.
#   - interpolate(0): zero-passthrough across all classes.
#   - interpolate identity: default tables round-trip raw → raw at cal points
#     and between cal points.
#   - interpolate at calibrated points: user cal tables recover the spinbox
#     LABEL value (Thetis PowerKernel returns idx*interval, not table[idx]).
#   - interpolate between calibrated points: piecewise-linear.
#   - interpolate above max: linear extrapolation of the last segment.
#   - interpolate None class: identity passthrough.
#   - interpolate below zero: identity on default tables.
#   - interpolate degenerate zero-span: defensive guard, no crash.
longpath_add_test(tst_pa_cal_profile)

# Regression test for the GUI-thread fexchange2 deadlock — verifies
# that RxDspWorker::processIqBatch runs on its own QThread when driven
# via Qt::QueuedConnection, and that posting a burst of batches does
# not block the sender. See src/models/RxDspWorker.h for the rationale.

# Regression test for rate-dependent audio jitter: RxDspWorker's drain
# chunk size must follow setBufferSizes() (per-rate Thetis formula
# in_size = 64 * rate / 48000), not a hardcoded 1024 that was correct
# only for 768 kHz and produced fexchange2 size mismatches at every
# other rate after PR #44 changed the default to 192 kHz.
longpath_add_test(tst_rx_dsp_worker_buffer_sizing)

# Phase 3F Sub-Epic I Task 4: RxDspWorker per-stream accumulation and
# per-slice fan-out.  processIqBatch used to accept a receiverIndex and
# ignore it, so a second DDC's samples landed in Slice A's accumulator and
# corrupted its audio.  Pins: streams accumulate independently, each drains
# at its own threshold, every slice bound to a stream is offered the drained
# chunk, and an unbound stream processes nothing.
longpath_add_test(tst_rx_dsp_worker_multi_slice)

# Rund 40 Faelle gegen ein rigctld-Doppel, viele warten auf die naechste
# Abfragerunde. Lokal ~60 s; auf dem macOS-Laeufer liefen schon am 08.11.
# (vor den Sprachspeicher-Faellen) die 120 s ab, danach 300 s.
# Nur im Shard, der ihn baut (Linux teilt die Pruefstaende auf).
#
# 2026-11-14 auf 900 s. Die 300 waren von Anfang an zu knapp, was erst
# auffiel, als sie rissen. Gemessen auf macOS Apple Silicon (2/2), jeweils
# derselbe Pruefstand als Test #90:
#
#   d6bad415d (vor #452)   Passed  237,60 s
#   240f4dc4d (mit #452)   Passed  254,49 s   <- 15 % Luft bei 300
#   3da9e7b16 (mit #453)   ***Timeout 300,09 s
#
# Es haengt also nicht, es war zu eng bemessen: bei 254 s Grundlast reisst
# eine Lastschwankung auf dem Laeufer die Frist, und zwar ohne dass sich
# am Pruefstand etwas geaendert haette (#453 fasst ihn gar nicht an).
# 900 s sind das Dreieinhalbfache der gemessenen Dauer, damit die naechsten
# Faelle nicht gleich wieder dagegen laufen. Wer die Dauer selbst senken
# will, muss an die Warterunden gegen das rigctld-Doppel -- das ist der
# Posten, nicht der Fensteraufbau.
if(TEST tst_cat_funkgeraet)
    set_tests_properties(tst_cat_funkgeraet PROPERTIES TIMEOUT 900)
endif()

# ── Phase 3F: per-ADC BPF decision reaches the wire (CT1IQI, PR #293) ─────────
# Half 1: RadioModel feeds AlexController::notifySlicesOnAdc from the live
#         slice set, grouped by the ADC each slice's stream sits on.
# Half 2: the resulting per-ADC decision composes the Alex0 / Alex1 HPF bytes
#         instead of the last-retuned receiver's frequency.
# Longpath-original: no Thetis port.
longpath_add_test(tst_alex_per_adc_bpf_wire)

# ── Phase 3F: an effective-BPF change pushes on its OWN trigger ───────────────
# republishAlexAdcSlices is the only producer of AlexRxBpf, and it used to be
# reachable only from requestDdcAssignment and ConnectionState::Connected.
# Neither of the two state changes that flip AlexAdcState::effective (operator
# override via FilterPolicyDialog, and wideband) went through either, so the
# bottom-bar CH label went amber while the preselector stayed where it was
# until the next VFO nudge. Thetis pushes from the setter with no deferral
# (console.cs:18793-18806 AlexHPFBypass [v2.10.3.15]).
# Also locks the re-entrancy guard: one band-crossing retune, one push.
# Longpath-original: no Thetis port.
longpath_add_test(tst_alex_bpf_policy_push)

# ── RF-safety: the Alex TX low-pass is selected from the TRANSMIT frequency ───
# P2RadioConnection::setReceiverFrequency used to recompute the LPF from the
# receive frequency it was handed, and both Alex words read that one mask, so
# the TX low-pass tracked whichever DDC was retuned last. Thetis keeps two
# masks and routes writes by intent (ChannelMaster/netInterface.c:682-726
# SetAlexLPFBits(bits, isTX, isMox) [v2.10.3.15]).
# Longpath-original: no Thetis port.
longpath_add_test(tst_alex_tx_lpf_source)

# ── Which frequency selects the Protocol 1 Alex low-pass (bank 10 C4) ────────
# P1 carries one low-pass field, not P2's Alex0/Alex1 pair, and Thetis fills it
# from prbpfilter -- the Alex0 struct (networkproto1.c:587-590 [v2.10.3.15];
# mi0bot's HL2 loop does the same at :1085-1088 [v2.10.3.14-beta1]). Alex0 is
# written by the `isMox || !isTX` arm of SetAlexLPFBits, so the byte carries the
# transmit selection while keyed and the RECEIVE selection while not. Longpath
# wrote it only from setTxFrequency and emitted it in both states, so with the
# transmitter bound to one slice and the operator listening on another the
# receiver sat behind the transmit band's low-pass. Also locks which receive
# frequency wins: UpdateAlexTXFilter takes the higher of the two on a board
# whose RX2 shares the Alex chain (console.cs:15487-15498 [v2.10.3.15]).
# Longpath-original: no Thetis port.
longpath_add_test(tst_p1_alex_lpf_word_source)

# Regression test for the 2026-08-26 band/mode persistence race (OE5SOS
# live bug report). See the test file's own header for the full story.
longpath_add_test(tst_radio_model_band_save_uses_slice_frequency)

# ── A band change must retune the DDC even while CTUN holds it ───────────────
# CTUN pins the DDC via ReceiverManager::m_ddcFreqLocked so a VFO nudge inside
# the pinned window moves the slice shift instead of retuning hardware. A band
# change is a different act: the allocator returns NewStream / RetunedStream,
# meaning the stream CENTRE moves, and bindSliceToStream pushed that through
# setReceiverFrequency, where the same lock swallowed it. Confirmed on a live
# HL2 2026-07-31: a 40 m to 60 m press gave outcome=NewStream then a dropped
# emit with ddcLocked=true, leaving the Alex high-pass and the receive
# low-pass on the previous band. Longpath-original: no Thetis port.
longpath_add_test(tst_band_change_retunes_ddc_in_ctun)

# ── Und die Gegenrichtung: ein CTUN-Zug macht den Zuteiler schal ──────────
# Der Zug schreibt Geraet, Widget und Versaetze -- aber nicht
# m_streamAllocator. Der naechste VFO-Schritt rechnet dann wieder gegen die
# alte Mitte, und der Ton sitzt um den gezogenen Betrag daneben, waehrend das
# Fahnenschild die richtige Frequenz zeigt. NereusSDR hat denselben Fall am
# 2026-10-25 behoben (f183631b9, ihre #648). Longpath-original: kein Port.
longpath_add_test(tst_ctun_zug_macht_zuteiler_schal)

# ── 2026-07-28 bench regression: click-to-tune always tuned flag A ──────────
# Two slices on one pan, and clicking the spectrum retuned the first one added
# however many times the operator selected the other flag. RadioModel's global
# active slice and PanadapterApplet's per-pan one were independent, and only
# the global one had a writer: PanadapterApplet::addSlice seeded the pan once
# and nothing ever moved it. MainWindow::sliceForPan reads the pan's value, so
# click-to-tune, the filter-edge drag, the CH tag and the pan TX pill all
# latched onto whichever slice reached the pan first.

# host it are left alone (per-pan controls act on their own pan), the slice-ID
# to list-POSITION conversion the activation wires need after a mid-list
# removal, and the migration invariant that no pan keeps pointing at a slice
# it no longer hosts.

# ── Tunable Notch Filter Task 1: the notch DB's tune frequency ─────────────
# WDSP maps absolute-Hz notch centres into a channel's passband with
# offset = tunefreq + shift (third_party/wdsp/src/nbp.c:192). tunefreq had
# never been pushed by anything in this tree, so every channel's
# notchdb.tunefreq sat at its construction default and the whole notch
# feature was unimplementable on top of it. Pins all four halves of design
# doc section 4: the pushed value is the hosting STREAM's centre and not the
# slice frequency (4.1/4.2, asserted for both the sole-owner and
# JoinedExisting outcomes); the shift reaches the notch database on the way
# back to zero and keeps its sign (4.3); the stream offset and RIT/DIG
# compose instead of clobbering each other in either writer order (4.4); and
# the connect-time DDC seed commands the stream centre (4.5).
# Source: Thetis console.cs:31940-31941 + radio.cs:1419-1420 [v2.10.3.15].
# Design: docs/architecture/2026-07-28-tunable-notch-filter-design.md 4.
longpath_add_test(tst_notch_tune_frequency)

# ── Phase 3F: WDSP channel-id allocation table ────────────────────────────
# WDSP keys every channel off one global array (channel.c:29) and OpenChannel
# overwrites a slot without closing it, so an id collision silently runs two
# wdspmain threads on the same channel and leaks the first one's buffers.
# Pins the upstream numbering (Thetis ChannelMaster/cmsetup.c:176-191 chid()
# and dsp.cs:926-944 WDSP.id() [v2.10.3.15]): RX slices occupy the low block,
# TX sits immediately above it, PS feedback above TX. Also pins the ceiling
# against BoardCapsTable::all() and drives RadioModel::openRxChannelPool to
# assert which ids it really opens.
longpath_add_test(tst_wdsp_channel_id_map)

# shared pan once (not once per slice), one stream can feed several pans at
# different zooms, and a wholesale rebuild drops stale subscriptions.

# ── Ins Logbuch SEHEN, vom Telefon aus (2026-10-04) ─────────────────────────
# tst_tci_logbuch_lesen: die Gegenstuecke zu log_qso: — log_last: und
#   log_dup:. Geprueft wird vor allem die ANZAHL in der Abschlusszeile: die
#   Seite zeigt eine Liste nur, wenn sie mit den gesendeten Zeilen
#   uebereinstimmt, weil eine kuerzere Liste wie ein kuerzeres Logbuch
#   aussieht und niemand es merkt. Dazu: juengster zuerst (auf einem Telefon
#   sieht man drei Zeilen), Ablehnung MIT Grund statt stumm (ein stummes Nein
#   ist auf einer Fernbedienung nicht von einem Defekt zu unterscheiden), und
#   der Deckel auf log_last: — 100000 Zeilen wuerden die Steuerleitung
#   fluten, hinter der jede Bedienung wartet.
longpath_add_test(tst_tci_logbuch_lesen)

#   EPERM) gegen "die Sendewarteschlange ist voll" (ENOBUFS). Grund: am
#   2026-10-04 verband Anvelina nicht, und der Dialog riet zur Netzstrecke,
#   waehrend in Wahrheit kein einziges Datagramm den Mac verliess. Werden die
#   beiden Faelle vertauscht, schickt die Meldung den Bediener an den falschen
#   Schalter.
longpath_add_test(tst_sendefehler_deutung)

# ── Eine Peilung vom Netz dreht echtes Metall (2026-10-05) ──────────────────
# tst_rotor_peilung: am anderen Ende der Leitung steht ein Mast mit einer
#   Antenne darauf. Drei Faelle, gegen die der Stand steht: `toDouble()` macht
#   aus "" und "abc" eine 0, und NULL GRAD IST NORD -- eine leere Zeile wuerde
#   die Antenne nach Norden drehen. NaN besteht jeden Bereichsvergleich, weil
#   jeder Vergleich mit NaN falsch ist. Und 360 ist dieselbe Richtung wie 0
#   und gehoert angenommen, 361 ist ein Tippfehler. Ausserhalb wird ABGELEHNT
#   statt umgerechnet: wer -90 schickt, hat sich vertan, und 270 Grad sind
#   eine andere Antwort als "ich habe mich vertan".
longpath_add_test(tst_rotor_peilung)

# ── Ein Feld, das die Zeile beendet, ist ein Einbruch (2026-10-07) ──────────
# tst_tci_feld: Longpaths eigene Antworten sind zeilenweise und durch Kommas
#   geteilt (`spot_zeile:`, `log_qso_zeile:`, `log_dup_ok:`) -- und die Felder
#   darin kommen NICHT von uns. `ruf`, `mode` und `quelle` stammen aus den
#   Spotquellen, und eine davon ist ein Telnet-Strom von einem fremden Rechner
#   im Internet; `call`, `band`, `mode` und die RST-Felder stammen aus der
#   ADIF-Datei, in der auch Einfuhren aus anderen Programmen liegen.
#   Ein Komma verschiebt alle folgenden Felder, ein Semikolon beendet die
#   Zeile -- und was danach kommt, liest die Gegenseite als NEUEN BEFEHL.
#   `derEinbruchGehtNichtMehr` fuehrt genau das vor: "OE5SOS;trx:0,true;".
longpath_add_test(tst_tci_feld)

# ── Den Rotor vom Telefon drehen -- und wann NICHT (2026-10-05) ─────────────
# tst_tci_rotor: am anderen Ende steht ein Mast, der minutenlang ohne Zutun
#   dreht, und niemand steht daneben. Darum dieselbe Strenge wie beim Senden:
#   ABFRAGEN darf jeder angemeldete Client (hinsehen bewegt nichts), DREHEN
#   nur mit TciAllowRemoteRotor=True -- ab Werk False, und wer die Vorgabe
#   versehentlich umdreht, faellt hier auf. Dazu: Ablehnung MIT Grund (ein
#   stummes Nein ist von einem Defekt nicht zu unterscheiden, und beim Rotor
#   sieht man ohnehin zehn Sekunden nichts), "kein Rotor" ist eine andere
#   Auskunft als "0 Grad", und eine unbrauchbare Peilung dreht NICHT nach
#   Norden.
longpath_add_test(tst_tci_rotor)
# ── Uhrendrift zwischen Funkgeraet und Tonkarte (#131, 2026-10-05) ──────────
# tst_audio_rate_matcher: das Regelgesetz aus Thetis' rmatch, auf die
#   Erzeugerseite geholt (CLAUDE.md verbietet eine Sperre im Tonrueckruf;
#   Thetis nimmt dort `cs_var`). Gemessen am 2026-09-27: der Ausgangsring
#   fuellt sich um 0,23 ms je Minute = rund 4 ppm, und laeuft mit dem
#   100-ms-Ring nach fuenf Stunden ueber.
#   Geprueft auf einer virtuellen Uhr ueber acht simulierte Stunden -- anders
#   ist acht Stunden Drift nicht zu pruefen. ENTSCHEIDEND ist die Gegenprobe
#   `ohneRegelungLaeuftDerRingUeber`: dieselbe Simulation mit festem
#   Verhaeltnis MUSS ueberlaufen, sonst belegt das gruene Ergebnis nur, dass
#   die Simulation zu gutmuetig ist.
#   Der Resampler und die Verdrahtung in den Tonweg sind NICHT dabei; die
#   kommen getrennt zur Durchsicht (Entwurf 2026-09-27, §4).
longpath_add_test(tst_audio_rate_matcher)

# ── Der Driftausgleich am Tonweg: die Verdrahtung (2026-10-06) ─────────────
# tst_rx_raten_angleich: das Regelgesetz steht in tst_audio_rate_matcher,
#   hier zaehlen die Zusagen der Verdrahtung. Die wichtigste: meldet der Bus
#   "Fuellstand unbekannt" (-1), gehen die Rahmen UNVERAENDERT durch, mit
#   demselben Zeiger. Nicht jeder Hintergrund hat einen Ring, den man fragen
#   kann (HAL-Shm, PipeWire, FIFO); wer -1 als 0 liest, sieht einen dauerhaft
#   leeren Puffer und regelt dagegen an, bis der Ton verstimmt ist.
#   Dazu die Gegenprobe ohne Ausgleich, die leerlaufen MUSS.
longpath_add_test(tst_rx_raten_angleich)
# tst_rx_drift_flex_schuebe (2026-11-11): an der FLEX-8400 zog der Ausgleich
#   -8000 ppm, weil der Bus nur zaehlte, was aus dem Ring kam. In Schueben
#   misst die Vorsteuerung dann ihr eigenes Verhaeltnis. Zaehlregel jetzt wie
#   Thetis xrmatchOUT: die volle Blocklaenge, auch bei Stille.
longpath_add_test(tst_rx_drift_flex_schuebe)

# ── Der Schalter fuer den Driftausgleich (2026-10-06) ───────────────────────
# tst_rx_drift_schalter: ein Haekchen, das nichts schaltet, ist schlimmer als
#   keines -- man legt es um, hoert hin, hoert keinen Unterschied und schliesst
#   daraus auf den Regler statt auf die Verdrahtung. Geprueft wird die
#   BEDIENUNG (toggle) und der Rueckweg: ab Werk aus, Klick schreibt die
#   Einstellung, und ein gesetzter Wert kommt beim naechsten Aufbau wieder
#   heraus.
longpath_add_test(tst_rx_drift_schalter)

# ── Vom Dateiende lesen darf keinen Kontakt ERFINDEN (2026-10-04) ───────────
# tst_logbuch_rueckschau: die App kann seit #184 loggen, aber nicht nachsehen.
#   Die letzten Kontakte kommen deshalb aus dem DATEIENDE (gemessen an Martins
#   Logbuch: 6,6 MB / 9271 Datensaetze — voller Durchlauf 4,1 ms, letzte 64 kB
#   0,04 ms). Genau daraus entsteht die Gefahr: ein Schnitt an einer
#   willkuerlichen Bytestelle beginnt mitten in einem Datensatz, und der
#   ADIF-Parser macht aus der hinteren Haelfte einen eigenen Kontakt — ein
#   Rufzeichen, das niemand gearbeitet hat, in einem Logbuch, das fuer Diplome
#   eingereicht wird. Der Stand fuehrt denselben Schnitt ohne und mit
#   Grenzschnitt vor; ohne entsteht der erfundene Datensatz wirklich.
#   Zweitens: eine Dupe-Warnung, die bei unbekanntem Band auf Verdacht wahr
#   wird, verhindert unbemerkt einen Kontakt, den es noch nicht gibt.
longpath_add_test(tst_logbuch_rueckschau)

# ── Spots fuer die App: was ins Bild passt, und NUR das (2026-10-05) ────────
# tst_spot_auswahl: Longpath sammelt Cluster, RBN, POTA und SpotCollector in
#   EINEM SpotModel; die Filter des Spot-Hub-Dialogs sitzen im Dialog, nicht
#   im Modell. RBN allein liefert in einer guten Stunde Hunderte Meldungen.
#   Ungefiltert ueber die TCI-Steuerleitung geschoben blockieren sie jede
#   Bedienung dahinter -- dieselbe Leitung traegt Frequenz, Betriebsart und
#   den Abbruch eines Sendewunsches. Gefiltert wird darum nach dem einzigen
#   Kriterium, das auf einem Telefon zaehlt: passt der Spot ins Bild.
#   Festgehalten: nichts von ausserhalb, abgelaufene fallen weg (der
#   Kehrbesen im SpotModel laeuft nur alle 30 s, und ein toter Spot kostet
#   einen Anruf ins Leere), und beim Kuerzen bleiben die NAHE DER MITTE.
longpath_add_test(tst_spot_auswahl)

# ── Der Weg der Spots durch den Server (2026-10-05) ─────────────────────────

# ── Qt-Prioritaet gegen QoS-Klasse (2026-10-03) ──────────────────────────────
# tst_audio_faden_qos: haelt die Regel fest, warum Audio-Faeden OHNE
#   QThread-Prioritaet starten. Qt setzt fuer eine ausdrueckliche Prioritaet
#   auf macOS die Ablaufparameter, und Darwin verweigert danach jede
#   QoS-Klasse (EPERM, Faden bleibt auf QOS_CLASS_UNSPECIFIED). Der
#   Sende-Faden lief deshalb ohne die Erhoehung, die er haben soll.
longpath_add_test(tst_audio_faden_qos)

#   nach seiner Auswahl hergibt (QFontInfo::fixedPitch). Vier Stellen nannten
#   je einen Namen, den es auf der laufenden Plattform nicht gibt ("SF Mono"
#   auf macOS -- loest hier auf zu .AppleSystemUIFont, also proportional --,
#   "Consolas" ausserhalb von Windows, dazu die CSS-Gattungsnamen
#   "monospace"/"sans-serif").
longpath_add_test(tst_schrift_dicktengleich)

# ── Issue #176: P1 EP6 status-frame parser was reading C0 bit 0 (mic_PTT)
# and emitting it as adcOverflow, causing the OVL badge to fire on every
# footswitch press while real ADC saturations went unreported.  This test
# constructs hand-crafted ep6 frames and verifies adcOverflow / micPttFromRadio
# fire from independent bytes per Thetis networkproto1.c:329 + 332-355
# [v2.10.3.13]:
#   ptt_in   = ControlBytesIn[0] & 0x1               (C0 bit 0)
#   case 0x00: adc[0] = ControlBytesIn[1] & 0x01     (C1 bit 0)
#   case 0x20: adc[0..2] = ControlBytesIn[1..3] & 0x01
longpath_add_test(tst_p1_adc_overflow_extraction)

# -- I2C-Antwort ist kein PTT (2026-09-23, Werkbank Runde 6): ein
#    Unterrahmen mit C0-Bit 7 traegt eine zurueckgegebene Adresse, keine
#    Zustandsbits -- die ungerade Adresse 0x7D hat das Geraet gekeyt. --
longpath_add_test(tst_p1_i2c_response_is_not_ptt)

# ── 3M-1a Task E.3: P1 MOX wire-byte emission ────────────────────────────────
# 13 tests covering:
#   1. Default state: C0 bit 0 clear before any setMox call.
#   2. setMox(true) → C0 bit 0 = 1 (bank-0 compose path).
#   3. setMox(false) → C0 bit 0 = 0.
#   4. Bank-0 address bits (7..1) remain 0 after setMox(true).
#   5. setMox() sets m_forceBank0Next so the next frame sends bank-0 ASAP.
#   6. Repeated setMox with same value still sets the flush flag (Codex P2).
#   7. Idempotent setMox(true) twice doesn't crash.
#   8. Round-trip true→false produces correct bytes.
#   9. Sub-frame 0 byte 11 (C0) carries the MOX bit.
#   10. Sub-frame 1 bank-1 C0 is not bit-0-only (address field differs).
#   11. HL2 firmware cross-check: bit 0 of eth_data[0] = PTT (dsopenhpsdr1.v:297).
#   12-13. Static composeCcBank0 helper agrees on bit position.
# Source: deskhpsdr/src/old_protocol.c:3595-3599 [@120188f].
longpath_add_test(tst_p1_mox_wire)
target_compile_definitions(tst_p1_mox_wire PRIVATE LONGPATH_BUILD_TESTS)

# ── Phase 3M-1a Task E.4: P1 T/R relay wire-byte snapshot ────────────────────
# 11 tests pin the Alex T/R relay bit (C3 bit 7 of bank 10, C0=0x12):
#   1. Default state: relay disengaged → bit 7 = 1.
#   2. setTrxRelay(true) → bit 7 = 0 (relay engaged, normal TX).
#   3. setTrxRelay(false) → bit 7 = 1 (relay disengaged, PA bypass).
#   4. Bank 10 C0 address stays 0x12 regardless of relay state.
#   5. setTrxRelay() sets m_forceBank10Next (Codex P2 flush pattern).
#   6. Repeated same-value call still sets the flush flag.
#   7. setTrxRelay(true) twice doesn't crash.
#   8. Round-trip: true → false → true produces correct bit values.
#   9. Alex HPF bits (C3 bits 6..0) are preserved across relay state changes.
#  10. HL2 firmware cross-check: bank 10 C0 maps to cmd_addr 6'h09 (informational).
#  11. Flush flag is set even when state doesn't change (false→false no-op).
# Source: deskhpsdr/src/old_protocol.c:2909-2910 [@120188f].
# Latent bug fixed: prior code wrote (m_paEnabled ? 0x80 : 0) — INVERTED.
longpath_add_test(tst_p1_trx_relay_wire)
target_compile_definitions(tst_p1_trx_relay_wire PRIVATE LONGPATH_BUILD_TESTS)

# ── Phase 3M-1c HL2 bench triage (2026-04-29): P1 setTxDrive wire-byte ───────
# 12 tests assert that bank 10 C1 carries the drive level set via setTxDrive().
#
# Bug fix: setTxDrive(int) was a no-op stub from 3M-1a; m_txDrive stayed at 0
# forever, so HL2 (and every P1 family member) silently shipped with zero TX
# drive and produced no RF on TUN or SSB.  PR #149 SSB voice bench tests ran
# only on ANAN-G2 (P2), masking the latent P1 stub.
#
# Standard and HL2 codec paths share the bank-10 C1 encoding per
# Thetis networkproto1.c:579 [v2.10.3.13] (= mi0bot @c26a8a4 line 1061);
# both codec paths exercised below.
longpath_add_test(tst_p1_drive_level_wire)

# ── Phase 3M-1a Task E.5: P1 RUNSTOP watchdog wire-byte snapshot ─────────────
# 12 tests assert that pkt[3] bit 7 of the RUNSTOP packet correctly encodes
# m_watchdogEnabled (inverted: 0=enabled, 1=disabled) per HL2 firmware.
#
# Wire cite: Hermes-Lite2/gateware/rtl/dsopenhpsdr1.v:399-400
#   watchdog_disable <= eth_data[7]; // Bit 7 can be used to disable watchdog
#
# deskhpsdr reference: deskhpsdr/src/old_protocol.c:3811 [@120188f]
#   buffer[3] = command; // no bit-7 OR -- watchdog always enabled implicitly
#
# Default changed: m_watchdogEnabled{false} -> {true} in RadioConnection.h
# so the first sendMetisStart() writes bit 7 = 0 (watchdog active), matching
# deskhpsdr implicit behavior.
longpath_add_test(tst_p1_watchdog_wire)
target_compile_definitions(tst_p1_watchdog_wire PRIVATE LONGPATH_BUILD_TESTS)

# ── Phase 3M-1a Task E.6: P2 sendTxIq wire-byte snapshot ─────────────────────
# 14 tests verify the 1444-byte UDP TX I/Q frame layout for P2RadioConnection:
#   1. Frame size = 1444 bytes (4-byte seq + 1440-byte payload).
#   2. Payload = 240×6 = 1440 bytes.
#   3. Sequence number starts at 0 and increments per frame.
#   4. Positive full-scale I (+1.0) → bytes 0x7F 0xFF 0xFF.
#   5. Negative full-scale Q (-1.0) → bytes 0x80 0x00 0x01.
#   6. Zero sample → all payload bytes zero.
#   7. Over-range positive (+2.0) clips to 0x7FFFFF.
#   8. Over-range negative (-2.0) clips to 0x800001.
#   9. Underrun (empty ring) → silence (all payload zeros).
#  10. Partial fill (60 of 240 samples) → remaining slots zero-padded.
#  11. Ring saturation: excess beyond capacity dropped, count ≤ capacity.
#  12. Sequence number does not reset across multiple calls.
#  13. Last sample (index 239) at correct offset (byte 1438).
#  14. No HL2 CWX LSB-clear in P2 — LSB=1 preserved.
# Porting from deskhpsdr/src/new_protocol.c:2795-2837 [@120188f].
longpath_add_test(tst_p2_tx_iq_wire)
target_compile_definitions(tst_p2_tx_iq_wire PRIVATE LONGPATH_BUILD_TESTS)

# ── P2 TX I/Q erst nach dem ersten Rahmen des Geraets (2026-09-30) ─────────
# Schein-Geraet auf 127.0.0.1:1024/1025/1029: vor dem ersten Status darf an
# 1029 nichts ankommen (ARP-Warteschlange von macOS verwarf sonst SendStart),
# danach laeuft der Takt. Ueberspringt sich, wenn die Ports belegt sind.
longpath_add_test(tst_p2_tx_iq_waits_for_radio)
target_compile_definitions(tst_p2_tx_iq_waits_for_radio PRIVATE LONGPATH_BUILD_TESTS)

# ── Issues #272 + #301: lazy SetupDialog page construction ──────────────────
# buildTree() used to construct all 55 setup pages from the SetupDialog ctor,
# blocking the Qt main thread on every Tools -> Setup open. That stall starved
# AudioEngine's drain timer (#301, repeated RX buffer on ANAN-10E/macOS) and on
# slower hosts outlasted the P1 ep6 watchdog, so the link dropped and the
# unclean audio teardown cascaded into a CLOCK_WATCHDOG_TIMEOUT BSOD
# (#272, HL2/Windows 11). Pages now build on first visit.
#
# Pins: registration builds nothing; selecting a leaf realizes exactly that
# leaf; revisits reuse the cached widget; a late-built page still reads live
# controller state and persists edits; and every cross-page wire that used to
# assume a sibling page already existed still fires (PA reset fan-out, parked
# TciServer pointer, CFC dialog request, per-SKU PA capability push).
longpath_add_test(tst_setup_dialog_lazy_pages)
target_compile_definitions(tst_setup_dialog_lazy_pages PRIVATE LONGPATH_BUILD_TESTS)

# MainWindow itself is too heavyweight to construct in a unit test (requires
# full RadioModel + WDSP + audio stack). Tests exercise the helper logic via
# standalone QLabel instances; Task 17 integration provides the live wiring.
longpath_add_test(tst_mainwindow_status_bar_safety)

# ── Upstream-Abgleich 2026-10-12: DEXP-Schwellenmarke ──────────────────────
# Die Marke auf dem DEXP-Pegelmesser kam vom Reglerbauwert (-50 dB) und
# wusste vom echten Schwellwert (TransmitModel::voxThresholdDb, im
# Mikrofonprofil gespeichert) nichts -- nach jedem Start und nach jeder

# ── Phase 3M-1a Task B.4: MoxController 6 phase signals (Codex P1) ───────────
# Verifies: each phase signal fires exactly once per real RX→TX/TX→RX walk;
# Order (RX→TX): txAboutToBegin → hardwareFlipped(true) → txReady → moxStateChanged(true);
# Order (TX→RX): txAboutToEnd → hardwareFlipped(false) → txaFlushed → rxReady → moxStateChanged(false);
# hardwareFlipped fires synchronously in setMox() (before rfDelay / keyUpDelay timers);
# Idempotent guard: repeated setMox(true/false) does NOT re-fire phase signals;
# Rapid toggle: cancelled rfDelay walk emits txAboutToBegin + hardwareFlipped(true)
#   but NOT txReady (slot never ran — cancelled timer);
# Codex P1: txAboutToBegin fires once per transition even when stateChanged fires 2×;
# Phase signals derived from chkMOX_CheckedChanged2 (console.cs:29311-29678 [v2.10.3.13]).
longpath_add_test(tst_mox_controller_phase_signals)

# ── Phase 3M-1a Task B.5: MoxController setTune(bool) slot ───────────────────
# Verifies: setTune(true) engages MOX, sets isManualMox=true, pttMode=Manual,
#   emits manualMoxChanged(true) and pttModeChanged(Manual) each exactly once;
# setTune(false) releases MOX, clears isManualMox (but NOT pttMode — F.1 resets it),
#   emits manualMoxChanged(false) exactly once;
# Longpath ordering: flags set BEFORE setMox(true) so phase-signal subscribers
#   see m_manualMox=true at txAboutToBegin time; flag cleared AFTER setMox(false)
#   so m_manualMox stays true during TX→RX phase signals (txAboutToEnd etc.);
# Idempotent guard: setTune(true) twice → manualMoxChanged fires only once;
# TUN→RAW-MOX combinations: direct setMox(true) then setTune(true) flips manualMox;
#   setTune(true) + setMox(false) leaves manualMox true; setTune(false) then clears;
# Codex P2 invariant: runMoxSafetyEffects fires on every setTune call (via setMox);
# Ports chkTUN_CheckedChanged (console.cs:29978-30157 [v2.10.3.13]) flag block.
longpath_add_test(tst_mox_controller_tune)

# ── Phase 3M-1b Task H.1: MoxController::setVoxEnabled voice-family mode-gate ─
# Verifies: setVoxEnabled(bool) + onModeChanged(DSPMode) + voxRunRequested(bool);
# Default state: no spurious emit at startup (m_voxEnabled=false, gated=false);
# LSB + VOX on → voxRunRequested(true) fires once;
# LSB + VOX off (after on) → voxRunRequested(false) fires once;
# CW mode + VOX on → voxRunRequested(true) does NOT fire (gate=false);
# LSB + VOX on, then mode change to CWL → voxRunRequested(false) fires once;
# CWL + VOX on (no emit), then mode change to LSB → voxRunRequested(true) fires once;
# Within-voice mode change (LSB→USB) while VOX on → no double emit;
# Repeat setVoxEnabled(true) in voice mode → no double emit (idempotent);
# All 8 voice-family modes (parametrized): + voxEnabled(true) → emits true;
# All 4 excluded modes (CWL/CWU/SPEC/DRM) + voxEnabled(true) → no true emit.
# Source: Thetis cmaster.cs:1039-1052 [v2.10.3.13] — CMSetTXAVoxRun.
longpath_add_test(tst_mox_controller_vox_enabled)

# ── Phase 3M-1b Task H.2: MoxController::setVoxThreshold mic-boost-aware scaling ─
# Verifies: setVoxThreshold(int dB) + onMicBoostChanged(bool) + setVoxGainScalar(float)
#           + voxThresholdRequested(double);
# NAN sentinel: first call always emits (primes WDSP regardless of default value);
# Boost OFF: threshold = pow(10, dB/20) only (no gain scaling);
# Boost ON, scalar 1.0: same result as boost-off (identity multiplication);
# Boost ON, scalar 0.5: threshold halved vs. boost-off;
# Gain scalar change (0.5→1.0) re-emits with new computed value;
# Mic-boost toggle (on→off→on) re-emits on each transition;
# Idempotent on emitted double: repeat setVoxThreshold(-40) → no second emit;
# Thetis cross-check Combo A: dB=-60, boost=off → 0.001 (exact);
# Thetis cross-check Combo B: dB=-20, boost=on, gain=0.5 → 0.05 (exact);
# Thetis cross-check Combo C: dB=0,   boost=on, gain=2.0 → 2.0 (exact);
# Boundary -80 dB: result > 0, not NaN/Inf, ≈ 1e-4;
# Boundary 0 dB: result ≈ 1.0, not NaN/Inf;
# Boost round-trip: off→on re-emits with the original scaled value.
# Source: Thetis cmaster.cs:1054-1059 + setup.cs:18908-18912 [v2.10.3.13].
longpath_add_test(tst_mox_controller_vox_threshold)

# ── Phase 3M-1c Tasks C.2 / C.3 / C.4: MoxController multicast Pre/Post signals ─
# Verifies the new 3-arg Pre/Post MOX state-change signals that future
# observers (PS form, TCI server, MeterPoller, audio recorder, etc.) plug into:
#   moxChanging(int rx, bool oldMox, bool newMox) — Pre, before m_mox commit;
#     subscribers can defensively freeze readings.
#   moxChanged (int rx, bool oldMox, bool newMox) — Post, after the timer
#     walk completes (parallels the existing 1-arg moxStateChanged boundary).
#
# Coverage:
#   §1.1  Pre fires before m_mox commit (isMox() inside slot returns OLD value);
#   §1.2  Post fires after timer walk with correct oldMox/newMox payload;
#   §1.3  Pre→Post ordering across a full RX→TX cycle;
#   §2.1  rx == 1 when rx2_enabled=false, vfobTx=false (default);
#   §2.2  rx == 1 when rx2_enabled=true,  vfobTx=false (RX2 alone is not a TX route);
#   §2.3  rx == 1 when rx2_enabled=false, vfobTx=true;
#   §2.4  rx == 2 when rx2_enabled=true,  vfobTx=true (TX off VFO-B via RX2);
#   §3.1  Idempotent setMox(false) on fresh controller emits neither Pre nor Post;
#   §4.1  TX→RX direction Post carries oldMox=true, newMox=false.
#
# The existing 1-arg moxStateChanged(bool) signal is unchanged — the 3-arg
# moxChanged is an additive overload (Pre/Post overlay), not a rename.
# Source: Thetis console.cs:29324 [v2.10.3.13] (MoxPreChangeHandlers, // MW0LGE_21k8);
#   console.cs:29677 [v2.10.3.13] (MoxChangeHandlers, // MW0LGE_21a).
longpath_add_test(tst_mox_controller_multicast_signals)

# ── Phase 3M-1b Task H.5: mic_ptt extraction from P1/P2 status frames ─────────
# Verifies end-to-end mic_ptt extraction and dispatch through MoxController.
#
# Prong A — RadioConnection-level extraction:
#   §A.1  P1: C0 bit 0 set in sub-frame 0 → micPttFromRadio(true)
#   §A.2  P1: C0 bit 0 set in sub-frame 1 only → micPttFromRadio(true) (OR logic)
#   §A.3  P1: C0 bit 0 clear in both sub-frames → micPttFromRadio(false)
#   §A.4  P1: ADC overload in C1 does NOT set micPtt (PTT is in C0, not C1)
#   §B.1  P2: raw[4] bit 0 set → micPttFromRadio(true)
#   §B.2  P2: raw[4] bit 0 clear → micPttFromRadio(false)
#   §B.3  P2: dot/dash bits (bits 1/2) only → micPttFromRadio(false)
#   §B.4  P2: ADC overload at raw[5] does NOT set micPtt (separate byte)
#
# Prong B — End-to-end (status frame → MoxController):
#   §C.1  P1 press → MoxController engaged, PttMode::Mic
#   §C.2  P1 release → MoxController released, PttMode retained
#   §C.3  P1 repeated press → idempotent (no double-emit)
#   §D.1  P2 press → MoxController engaged, PttMode::Mic
#   §D.2  P2 release → MoxController released, PttMode retained
#   §D.3  P2 repeated press → idempotent (no double-emit)
#   §E.1  P1: consecutive false frames both emit (unconditional emission)
#   §E.2  P2: consecutive false packets both emit (unconditional emission)
#
# Source: Thetis console.cs:25426 [v2.10.3.13] (PollPTT mic_ptt bit);
#   networkproto1.c:329 [v2.10.3.13] (P1 ptt_in bit);
#   network.c:686-689 [v2.10.3.13] (P2 ptt_in byte/bit);
#   deskhpsdr/src/new_protocol.c:2525 [@120188f] (P2 cross-ref).
longpath_add_test(tst_mox_controller_mic_ptt_extraction)
target_compile_definitions(tst_mox_controller_mic_ptt_extraction PRIVATE LONGPATH_BUILD_TESTS)

# after createTxChannel because create_dexp had never been ported into

# ── Phase 3M-1b Task E.1: AudioEngine::pullTxMic ─────────────────────────────
# Verifies the TX-mic pull accessor that PcMicSource (Phase F.1) will tap:
#   nullBus_returnsZero       — no bus configured → 0
#   zeroN_returnsZero         — n <= 0 guard → 0
#   nullDst_returnsZero       — null dst guard → 0
#   emptyBus_returnsZero      — bus open but no data → 0
#   int16Stereo_returnsSampleCount        — 2 stereo frames → 2
#   int16StereoToFloat32Mono_correctConversion — left/32768, right discarded
#   float32StereoToMono_correctPassthrough     — left taken, right discarded
#   float32Mono_fullTransfer  — mono bus, all 3 samples pass through
#   partialBus_returnsActualCount — 1 frame available, asked for 4 → 1
#
# Uses FakeAudioBus injected via LONGPATH_BUILD_TESTS seam setTxInputBusForTest.
# Pre-code review: §0.3 (PcMicSource arch).
longpath_add_test(tst_audio_engine_pull_tx_mic)
target_compile_definitions(tst_audio_engine_pull_tx_mic PRIVATE LONGPATH_BUILD_TESTS)

# ── Phase 3M-1b Task G.1: P1 mic_boost wire-byte snapshot ────────────────────
# 14 tests pin the mic_boost bit (C2 bit 0 = 0x01) of bank 10 (C0=0x12) in
# P1RadioConnection::setMicBoost():
#   1.  Default state: C2 bit 0 = 0.
#   2.  setMicBoost(true)  → C2 bit 0 = 1.
#   3.  setMicBoost(false) → C2 bit 0 = 0.
#   4.  Bit 6 (0x40) always set regardless of mic_boost.
#   5.  No spillover into C2 bits 7, 5..2, 1.
#   6.  Bank 10 C0 address stays 0x12.
#   7.  setMicBoost sets m_forceBank10Next (Codex P2 flush pattern).
#   8.  Repeated same-value call still sets the flush flag.
#   9.  Idempotency: setMicBoost(true) twice doesn't crash.
#  10.  Round-trip: true → false produces correct bit values.
#  11.  Codec path (Standard): C2 bit 0 set/cleared correctly.
#  12.  Codec path (Standard): C2 bit 0 clear after false.
#  13.  Codec path (HL2): C2 bit 0 set (FW ignores, but bit is correct).

#  13.  Codec path (HL2): setMicTipRing(false) → C1 bit 4 set (FW ignores).
#  14.  setMicTipRing(false) does NOT touch C1 bits 0-3 — cross-bit guard.

# 14 tests pin the mic_bias bit (C1 bit 5 = 0x20) of bank 11 (C0=0x14)
# in P1RadioConnection::setMicBias().
# Polarity: on=true (bias on) → wire bit 1; on=false (bias off) → wire bit 0.
# Reuses m_forceBank11Next + captureBank11ForTest infrastructure from G.3.
#   1.  Default state: C1 bit 5 = 0 (default m_micBias=false).
#   2.  setMicBias(true)  → C1 bit 5 = 1 (bias on → wire bit set).
#   3.  setMicBias(false) → C1 bit 5 = 0 (bias off → wire bit clear).
#   4.  Round-trip: true → false → bit clears after set.
#   5.  Bank 11 C0 address stays 0x14.
#   6.  setMicBias(true) does NOT clobber C1 bits 0-3 (preamp).
#   7.  setMicBias(true) does NOT touch C1 bit 4 (G.3 mic_trs, cross-bit guard).
#   8.  setMicBias(true) produces C1 = 0x20 in default state (only bit 5).

#  11.  Idempotency: setMicBias(true) twice → bit stays 1.
#  12.  Codec path (Standard): setMicBias(true) → C1 bit 5 set.
#  13.  Codec path (Standard): setMicBias(false) → C1 bit 5 clear.
#  14.  Codec path (HL2): setMicBias(true) → C1 bit 5 set (FW ignores).
# Source: Thetis ChannelMaster/networkproto1.c:597 [v2.10.3.13].

# ── Phase 3M-1b Task G.5: P1 mic_ptt wire-byte snapshot ─────────────────────
# 16 tests pin the mic_ptt bit (C1 bit 6 = 0x40, INVERTED) of bank 11 (C0=0x14)
# in P1RadioConnection::setMicPTT().
# POLARITY INVERSION: enabled=true → wire bit CLEAR; enabled=false → wire bit SET.
# Reuses m_forceBank11Next + captureBank11ForTest infrastructure from G.3.
#   1.  Default state: C1 bit 6 = 1 (PTT disabled by default — m_micPTT=false → !false=1).
#   2.  setMicPTT(true)  → C1 bit 6 = 0 (PTT enabled → disable-flag cleared).
#   3.  setMicPTT(false) → C1 bit 6 = 1 (PTT disabled → disable-flag set).
#   4.  Round-trip true → false.
#   5.  Bank 11 C0 address must be 0x14.
#   6.  setMicPTT(true) does NOT clobber C1 bits 0-3 (preamp).
#   7.  setMicPTT(true) does NOT touch C1 bits 4 or 5 (G.3/G.4 bits).
#   8.  setMicPTT sets m_forceBank11Next (Codex P2 flush pattern).
#   9.  Flush flag set even when value doesn't change (Codex P2).
#  10.  Idempotency: setMicPTT(true) twice → bit stays 0.
#  11.  Codec path (Standard): setMicPTT(true) → C1 bit 6 clear.
#  12.  Codec path (Standard): setMicPTT(false) → C1 bit 6 set.
#  13.  Codec path (HL2): setMicPTT(true) → C1 bit 6 clear (FW ignores).
#  14.  setMicPTT(true) does NOT touch C1 bits 0-3 — cross-bit guard.
#  15.  G.3+G.4+G.5 composition: mic_trs(inverted)+mic_bias+mic_ptt(inverted) → C1 & 0x70 == 0x70.
#  16.  G.3+G.4 composition sub-case: mic_trs+mic_bias → C1 & 0x30 == 0x30.
# Source: Thetis ChannelMaster/networkproto1.c:597-598 [v2.10.3.13].

# =============================================================================
# Covers:

#   3a. Non-HL2 fresh connect: default Pc.
#   3b. Non-HL2 fresh connect: not locked.
#   4.  Non-HL2 with stored Radio: loads Radio (no override).
#   5.  Lock prevents setMicSource(Radio) on HL2 → stays Pc.
#   6.  Lock released on non-HL2 → setMicSource(Radio) succeeds.
#   7.  isMicSourceLocked() reflects hasMicJack for both boards.
#   8.  Reconnect HL2 → non-HL2: lock released, Radio allowed.
#
# LONGPATH_BUILD_TESTS required for RadioModel test seams:
#   setCapsHasMicJackForTest, simulateConnectLoadForTest,
#   simulateDisconnectForTest.
longpath_add_test(tst_radio_model_mic_source_hl2_force)
target_compile_definitions(tst_radio_model_mic_source_hl2_force PRIVATE LONGPATH_BUILD_TESTS)

# ── Task 4.3: Filter Impulse Cache toggle persistence ─────────────────────────
# AppSettings round-trip for DspOptionsCacheImpulse and
# DspOptionsCacheImpulseSaveRestore. Both default to "False". The WdspEngine
# reads these flags at finishInitialization() and shutdown(); they take effect

#   cache_impulse_default_false — remove key → default is "False"

#   cache_impulse_round_trip_false — set "False" → reads back "False"
#   cache_save_restore_default_false — remove key → default is "False"
#   cache_save_restore_round_trip_true — set "True" → reads back "True"
#   cache_save_restore_round_trip_false — set "False" → reads back "False"
#   cache_impulse_and_save_restore_are_independent — orthogonal flags
longpath_add_test(tst_dsp_options_persistence)

# ── Task 4.5: Warning icon logic — Thetis validity rules ──────────────────────
# Ported from Thetis console.cs:38797-38807 + setup.cs:22391-22400 [v2.10.3.13].
#
# Validity rules: warnings show when settings differ across modes:
#   bufferSizeDifferentRX  = !(phone==fm && fm==cw && cw==dig)  (4-way RX)
#   bufferSizeDifferentTX  = !(phone==fm && fm==dig)            (3-way TX; no CW TX)
#   (same pattern for filter size; filter type uses separate RX/TX type combos)
#   pbWarningBufferSize.Visible = bufferSizeDifferentRX || bufferSizeDifferentTX
#   pbWarningFilterSize.Visible = filterSizeDifferentRX || filterSizeDifferentTX
#   pbWarningBufferType.Visible = filterTypeDifferentRX || filterTypeDifferentTX
#
# Tests the static helpers DspOptionsPage::comboValuesDiffer4 / comboValuesDiffer3
# and the composite OR logic for each warning label.
#
#   differ4_all_equal_returns_false — 4 equal combos → false
#   differ4_one_different_returns_true — 1 of 4 differs → true
#   differ4_two_different_returns_true — 2 of 4 differ → true
#   differ4_all_different_returns_true — all 4 differ → true
#   differ3_all_equal_returns_false — 3 equal combos → false
#   differ3_one_different_returns_true — 1 of 3 differs → true
#   differ3_two_different_returns_true — 2 of 3 differ → true
#   buffer_warn_fires_on_rx_side_only — RX side differs, TX uniform → warning
#   buffer_warn_fires_on_tx_side_only — FM differs → both sides diverge → warning
#   buffer_warn_hidden_when_all_match — all 4 equal → no warning
#   filter_size_same_logic_as_buffer — all equal: no warn; CW changed: warn
#   filter_type_rx_3_of_4_equal_fires — 3 of 4 RX types equal, 1 differs → warn
#   filter_type_all_rx_equal_no_warning — all RX equal AND all TX equal → no warn
longpath_add_test(tst_dsp_options_warning_logic)

# ── Task 2.1: Detector + Averaging split (handwave fix from 3G-8) ─────────
# Ported from Thetis specHPSDR.cs:302-415 [v2.10.3.13] DetTypePan / DetTypeWF
# / AverageMode / AverageModeWF integer codes.
#
# tst_detector_modes: pure math over SpectrumWidget::applyDetector().
#   Peak returns max in window; Rosenfell alternates max/min per pixel;
#   Average computes arithmetic mean; Sample takes first bin;
#   RMS computes root-mean-square; empty input / zero outputBins are no-ops.
#   Uses QTEST_APPLESS_MAIN (no QApplication, WDSP-free).
#
# Note: tst_averaging_modes was retired with the SpectrumWidget::applyAveraging
# static helper.  Frame averaging is now performed by SpectrumAvenger
# (verbatim WDSP analyzer.c:464-554 [v2.10.3.13] port) on linear-power
# display pixels; coverage of that path is exercised end-to-end via the
# running app.  Direct SpectrumAvenger unit tests are a follow-up item.
longpath_add_test(tst_detector_modes)

# ── Braucht ein AKTIVES Fenster, also allein ─────────────────────────
#
# Gemessen am 2026-08-23, nach drei falschen Reparaturen an diesem
# Prueflauf. Zwei Sonden zusammen ergaben:
#
#   Gutfall:    Fenster aktiv: true  · Menues jetzt: 2
#   Fehlerfall: Fenster aktiv: false · Menues jetzt: 0
#
# Der Notch wird in beiden Faellen gefunden, die Geometrie stimmt. Der
# Ablauf im Fehlerfall: new QMenu(this) -> popup() -> das Fenster ist
# nicht aktiv -> das Aufklappmenue verliert sofort den Fokus -> es
# schliesst -> WA_DeleteOnClose -> es ist weg, ehe der Test hinsieht.
#
# Das ist keine Schwaeche unseres Codes, sondern eine Eigenschaft
# DIESER PRUEFUNG: sie braucht ein aktives Fenster. Unter "ctest -j8"
# streiten acht Oberflaechentests darum, und einer verliert.
#
# RUN_SERIAL nimmt ihm die Gegner. Das ist die richtige Antwort auf
# "der Test braucht etwas Exklusives" — und nicht die vierte Runde
# Symptombehandlung im Testcode.
if(TEST tst_notch_removable_offline)
    set_tests_properties(tst_notch_removable_offline PROPERTIES RUN_SERIAL TRUE)
endif()
longpath_add_test(tst_notch_menu_wins_over_pan_menu)
# Braucht ein aktives Fenster, aus demselben Grund wie
# tst_notch_removable_offline — dort ausfuehrlich begruendet.

    set_tests_properties(tst_notch_menu_wins_over_pan_menu PROPERTIES RUN_SERIAL TRUE)
endif()

longpath_add_test(tst_callsign_cache)

# -- Turning a measured sweep into "add 22 cm to each leg". The tests
#    that matter are the refusals: a Touchstone header with no unit is
#    NOT read as gigahertz (the standard's default), and the full-wave
#    anti-resonance is NOT offered as something to trim towards --
longpath_add_test(tst_antenna_sweep)

# ── The window that stayed on the previous band ─────────────────────────────
# A 40 m sweep defaulted the target to 7.100; the 20 m sweep that followed was
# then labelled and tabulated as 40 m, and could not recover because the window
# re-defaults the target to the middle of whatever band it is shown. Pins the
# invariant: the band shown is one the sweep covers.
longpath_add_test(tst_swr_curve_band)

# ── Die Achse, mit Zahlen statt mit Augenmass geprueft ──────────────────────
# Ein Bereichs-Sweep war viermal unlesbar, und jedes Mal habe ich das ZEICHNEN
# verbessert, obwohl die ACHSE schuld war: auf 1,8-30 MHz linear ist 40 m
# 0,7 % der Breite, etwa zehn Bildpunkte. Diese Frage hat eine Zahl als
# Antwort, und eine Zahl laesst sich zusichern.
longpath_add_test(tst_swr_curve_panels)

# ── Der dritte Zustand: gemessen, echt, und nicht mehr dieses Band ──────────
# Waehrend eines laufenden Sweeps nannte der Kopf des Antennenfensters das neue
# Band, waehrend Kurve, Kacheln, Spanne und Tabelle darunter weiter das vorige
# beschrieben -- beide Haelften aktuell, ueber verschiedene Baender. Ursache war
# nicht ein vergessener Aufruf, sondern ein fehlender Zustand: die Auswertung
# kannte nur "Sweep" und "kein Sweep". Sichert die vier Regeln (anderes Band
# daempft, gleiches nicht, Fehllauf bleibt, echte Messung loest) und die
# Grenze, die kein Design ueberschreibt: SWR-Rot und Grenzlinie daempfen nie.
longpath_add_test(tst_swr_analysis_superseded)

# -- Theme substitution. 1733 Hex-Literale in 130 Dateien, 1141 davon in

# ── 2026-09-06 race fix: setXxxTap(nullptr, ...) vs. an in-flight write ──────
# Code review found that AudioEngine::setQsoTap / setAsrTap /
# setWavRecordTap / setRttyTap only ever stored two plain atomics, with no
# handshake against a rxBlockReady() call already past the tap-pointer load
# on the real audio thread — a caller that frees the AudioTapRing right
# after disabling the tap (e.g. QsoRecorderController's dtor, "Erst den
# Abgriff loesen, dann sterben") could race a still-running write() into
# freed memory. Fixed via a per-tap busy counter (writeToTapIfCurrent /
# waitForTapQuiescence in AudioEngine.cpp) that setXxxTap(nullptr, ...)
# drains before returning. This test uses the LONGPATH_BUILD_TESTS-only
# setTapWriteDelayHookForTest seam to hold a simulated audio thread inside
# that exact window and asserts setXxxTap(nullptr, ...) blocks for the
# delay's duration instead of returning immediately — deterministic and
# CI-safe, unlike actually racing a real use-after-free.
longpath_add_test(tst_audio_engine_tap_teardown_race)

longpath_add_test(tst_qso_recorder_controller)

# -- Was die RxApplet von der VFO-Flagge geerbt hat. Fuenf Gruppen
#    lebten NUR dort; die Flagge faellt ersatzlos weg. Gingen sie still
#    mit, faende es niemand: es gibt keine Fehlermeldung fuer eine
#    Bedienung, die es nicht mehr gibt, und die Pillen unten sind
#    ANZEIGEN, keine Schalter. Besonders Lautstaerke und Stumm -- ohne
#    sie haette das Programm weder das eine noch das andere, weil die
#    „TitleBar master volume", auf die der alte Kommentar verwies, in
#    Longpath nie gebaut wurde --
longpath_add_test(tst_rx_applet_inherited)

# -- Die Quellenliste der Instrumente und die Kennlinie der
#    Empfangsskala. Der Kern ist die S-Stauchung, und zwar als
#    STUETZSTELLEN: S0 auf 0, S9 auf 0,66, S9+60 auf 1. Eine
#    Monotonieprüfung haette jede krumme Kennlinie durchgelassen,
#    solange sie nur steigt -- und eine Skala, auf der S9 nicht dort
#    sitzt, wo der Betreiber sie gezeichnet hat, ist eine andere Skala.
#    Dazu: unter S9 gleichmaessig, darueber gestaucht, und die
#    Stauchung eine einmalige Aenderung der Teilung statt einer
#    fortlaufenden Kruemmung --
longpath_add_test(tst_reading_source)

# -- Die Geometrie der Instrumente: Anteil -> Ort, fuer Bogen und
#    Gerade. Laufen die beiden auseinander, zeigen zwei Instrumente
#    denselben Wert an verschiedenen Stellen. Enthaelt die Pruefung,
#    dass 90 Grad NACH OBEN zeigt -- ein Vorzeichenfehler dort stellt
#    jedes Zeigerinstrument auf den Kopf, und zwar so, dass es auf den
#    ersten Blick plausibel aussieht --
longpath_add_test(tst_instrument_spine)

# -- Ein Instrument ohne Messung darf keine Zahl zeigen. Befund vom
#    2026-08-17: die Stehwelle stand ohne Verbindung auf 1.00, Spitze
#    1.00, Zeiger am Anschlag. 1,00 ist ein hervorragendes SWR -- ein
#    Instrument, das ohne Messung ein hervorragendes Ergebnis anzeigt,
#    luegt in die beruhigende Richtung, und bei einer Anzeige, an der
#    man abliest, ob man senden darf, ist das die falsche --
longpath_add_test(tst_instrument_no_reading)

# -- Das Rechtsklickmenue der Instrumente. Bis zum 2026-08-18 gab es
#    ZWEI Wege, eine Empfangsquelle zu waehlen: die Kennung aus
#    ReadingSource (jedes Messwerkzeug) und die eigene RxMode-Liste im
#    Rechtsklick der analogen S-Meter-Anzeige. Zwei Listen fuer dieselbe
#    Entscheidung laufen auseinander, und sie hatten es schon getan --
#    "Max Bin" stand nur in der zweiten. Der Test haelt fest, dass das
#    Menue AUS der einen Liste kommt und nicht daneben --
longpath_add_test(tst_instrument_menu)

# -- Das Frequenz-Widget BEDIENT, es zeigt nicht nur. Auflage des
#    Betreibers: ohne Rad und Klick-Eingabe naehme das Ausblenden der
#    VFO-Flagge das Abstimmen mit der Maus weg. Geprueft wird, dass das
#    Rad ueber einer Ziffer GENAU DEREN Dekade dreht (alle acht
#    einzeln) und dass das Widget sich nicht selbst verstellt, sondern
#    den Wunsch meldet -- so laeuft die Aenderung ueber denselben Weg
#    wie jede andere Abstimmung --
longpath_add_test(tst_frequency_instrument)

# -- Taking the coax back out. Loss flatters the SWR, but the one that
#    ruins a trim is the phase rotation: five metres of RG-58 left the
#    dipole with exactly one crossing in the band, a FALLING one at
#    45 ohms, which the first nearestResonance() returned as the answer --
longpath_add_test(tst_feedline)

# -- Learning how THIS antenna behaves from two sweeps either side of a
#    known cut. The textbook f proportional to 1/L is free-space; the
#    worked case came out at L^-0.5, which doubles the wire needed. The
#    refusals matter more than the arithmetic --
longpath_add_test(tst_trim_session)

longpath_add_test(tst_coupler_zero)

# ── Plan 4 Task 6 (D8): TX filter debounce + per-mode IQ-space mapping ────────
# 9 tests: debounce coalesces rapid calls, USB/LSB/AM/DIGU/DIGL/FM/DSB/CWL

# ── Issue #175 Task 5: HL2 audio-volume formula in computeAudioVolume ─────────
# 5 tests covering the mi0bot HL2 branch ((hl2Power * gbb/100) / 93.75)
# placed BEFORE the gbb >= 99.5 sentinel so HL2 HF bands actually use the
# formula instead of falling into the legacy linear path:
#   §1 HL2 40m (gbb=100) at full   -> clamps to 1.0
#   §2 HL2 40m (gbb=100) at zero   -> 0.0 (sliderWatts<=0 short-circuit)
#   §3 HL2 40m (gbb=100) at mid    -> 50/93.75 exactly
#   §4 HL2 6m  (gbb=38.8) at full  -> (99 * 0.388) / 93.75
#   §5 ANAN-100 model on a Bypass profile -> legacy linear fallback (0.5),
#                                           differs from the HL2 formula value.
# Source: mi0bot-Thetis console.cs:47775-47778 [v2.10.3.13-beta2].
longpath_add_test(tst_transmit_model_hl2_audio_volume)

# ── v0.4.1 hotfix: RadioModel pushes hardware HPSDRModel to ReceiverManager ──
# 5 tests pinning the contract that RadioModel propagates m_hardwareProfile.
# model into BOTH TransmitModel and ReceiverManager. The ReceiverManager
# push was missing in v0.4.0 → P1CodecStandard saw the default HPSDR enum
# value, fell through its model switch's default branch, and emitted an
# empty PsDdcConfig — keeping PsccPump inactive on every Hermes / ANAN-10 /
# ANAN-10E / ANAN-100 / ANAN-100B / AnvelinaPro3-on-P1. HL2 / G2 / Saturn
# were unaffected because their codecs ignore the model parameter.
longpath_add_test(tst_radio_model_hpsdr_model_push)
target_compile_definitions(tst_radio_model_hpsdr_model_push PRIVATE LONGPATH_BUILD_TESTS)

# ── v0.4.1 hotfix: setAttenuator + setPreamp bank-11 flush-flag parity ───────
# 7 tests pinning the Codex P2 flush-flag pattern on the two bank-11 setters
# that v0.4.0 missed (peer setters setMicTipRing / setMicBoost / setLineIn /
# setUserDigOut / setMicPTTDisabled / setPuresignalRun all set
# m_forceBank11Next = true before their idempotent guards). Without the
# flush flag, RX step-att and preamp changes wait up to ~22 ms for the next
# natural bank-11 visit in the round-robin instead of landing within ≤2.6 ms.
longpath_add_test(tst_p1_step_att_flush_flag)
target_compile_definitions(tst_p1_step_att_flush_flag PRIVATE LONGPATH_BUILD_TESTS)

# ── v0.4.1 hotfix: StepAttenuatorController cross-thread connection pushes ───
# 2 tests pinning the contract that controller.setAttenuation() and
# controller.setPreampMode() marshal to the connection thread via
# QMetaObject::invokeMethod, NOT direct method calls. Direct cross-thread
# slot calls bypass Qt's queued dispatch, write connection-thread-owned
# state from the wrong thread, and (on weaker memory models like ARM64)
# can delay or pair the write with stale reads. The other invokeMethod
# call sites in StepAttenuatorController (setTxStepAttenuation paths at
# lines 364/469/535/557) already follow this pattern; this test pins
# parity for the RX-side setters that v0.4.0 missed.
longpath_add_test(tst_step_att_cross_thread)
target_compile_definitions(tst_step_att_cross_thread PRIVATE LONGPATH_BUILD_TESTS)

# ── v0.4.1 hotfix: P1CodecStandard bank 16 ps_run wire (smoking gun) ─────────
# 5 tests pinning that P1CodecStandard's bank 16 emits the puresignal_run bit
# on C2 bit 6 — the same fix that landed in P1CodecHl2 on 2026-05-07 (PR #212
# follow-up bench-fix) but never propagated to P1CodecStandard. Without this
# bit, the radio FPGA fires the cmaster.cs bookkeeping but the PA-loopback
# physical path stays disengaged on Hermes-class boards (Hermes / ANAN-10 /
# ANAN-10E / ANAN-100 / ANAN-100B), causing calcc to reach LCOLLECT but never
# see usable feedback samples. Verified against bench bundle from a friend's
# ANAN-10E running v0.4.1-rc1: TX envelope 0.20 (-14 dBFS) but FB envelope
# 0.0003 (-70 dBFS, ADC noise floor) for 1361 pumped blocks straight.
# Thetis source: networkproto1.c:657-666 [v2.10.3.13] case 16.
longpath_add_test(tst_p1_bank16_pursignal_run)
target_compile_definitions(tst_p1_bank16_pursignal_run PRIVATE LONGPATH_BUILD_TESTS)

# ── Phase 3J-2 Task D1: SpotModel (TCI-keyed spot sink) ────────────────────
# Pins the contract that SpotModel is a QMap<int, SpotData> sink
# keyed by monotonic spot index, with a TCI-keyed update API:
#   void applySpotStatus(int index, const QMap<QString,QString>& kvs);
# recognising 12 keys (callsign, rx_freq, tx_freq, mode, color,
# background_color, source, spotter_callsign, comment, timestamp,
# lifetime_seconds, priority) and decoding the TCI 0x7F (DEL)
# wire-format quirk to a single ASCII space in callsign and comment.
# Six signals: spotAdded / spotUpdated / spotRemoved / spotsCleared /
# spotsRefreshed / spotTriggered. Seven tests:
#   initialState (empty model reports zero spots), applySpotStatusAddsNew
#   (first applySpotStatus for an index emits spotAdded), applySpotStatusUpdatesExisting
#   (subsequent applySpotStatus emits spotUpdated and preserves prior
#   fields not in the update), appliesAllTwelveKeys (every recognised
#   key round-trips), decodes0x7FAsSpace (TCI wire-format quirk decoded
#   in callsign + comment), removeSpotEmitsSignal (removeSpot emits
#   spotRemoved and erases the row), clearEmitsSignal (clear emits
#   spotsCleared once and empties the map). Source: AetherSDR
#   src/models/SpotModel.{h,cpp} [@0cd4559]. First task of Phase D
#   (Models).
longpath_add_test(tst_spot_model)

# ── Phase 3J-2 Task D2: SpotTableModel + BandFilterProxy ───────────────────
# Pins the contract that SpotTableModel is a QAbstractTableModel with
# 8 columns (Time, Freq, DxCall, Comment, Spotter, Band, Mode, Source)
# bounded at 500 spots; BandFilterProxy is a QSortFilterProxyModel
# that hides spots whose band is in m_hiddenBands. Eight tests:
#   initialState (empty model reports zero rows + 8 columns),
#   addSpotIncrementsRowCount (addSpot grows the row count by one),
#   columnCountIsEight (ColCount sentinel == 8), dataRoundTrip
#   (DxCall column for a freshly-added spot returns the inserted
#   callsign), freqAtRowMatchesSource (freqAtRow(row) returns the
#   inserted freqMhz), setMaxSpotsBounds (with maxSpots=3, adding
#   10 spots leaves rowCount() == 3), bandFilterHidesMatchingBand
#   (proxy sees both spots until setBandVisible("20m", false), then
#   only the 40m spot survives), bandFilterShowsAfterUnhide (toggling
#   visibility back to true restores the row). Source: AetherSDR
#   src/gui/DxClusterDialog.h:33-75 + src/gui/DxClusterDialog.cpp:75-226
